The suite is SSR-only, so these were checked instead by rendering the component with the real built CSS to a static page and looking at it (folded footer, unfolded footer, collapsed rail); that proves layout and paint, not behaviour. |
| **P4-62** ResumeAgent ack card: outcome visible without expanding | CLAUDE (visual-design) | 3 | ✅ 2026-08-02 | Operator-directed, not backlog-generated. Operator saw a `ResumeAgent` card whose entire result was the escaped JSON `{"success":true,"message":"Resumed \"@Ramanujan\" …"}`, reachable only by expanding. Three compounding causes, all fixed: `deriveToolFamily` had no case for the tool (`transcriptProjector.ts:1361-1365`) so it fell to `'other'` — header word `Tool`, target the bare tool name, and the only grey mark in a 13-family set; the body renders only when expanded (`TranscriptView.tsx:921-930`, `defaultExpanded` false on success); and family `other` routes to `PlainLinesBody`, printing `jsonStringify(output)` (`src/tools/ResumeAgentTool/ResumeAgentTool.tsx:93-99`) verbatim. **Built:** a new `'agent-control'` family (`ResumeAgent`, `SendMessage` — constants verified at `src/tools/ResumeAgentTool/constants.ts:1` and `src/tools/SendMessageTool/constants.ts:1`) rendering ◇ + word `Agent` + `#a78bfa`, deliberately NOT `'agent'` because `ToolCard` routes that family to `AgentToolCard`, whose body reads spawn-shaped input these tools lack; a header target from each tool's real recipient key (`agentId`/`to`); a collapsed one-line peek in `collapsedExtra`, the slot `FrameEShell` reserved for exactly this and the prototype never filled (`Messages.jsx:93` — "bash tail peek, agent one-liner"), same band and 11px mono as `BashTailPeek`; and an expanded body in `AgentCompletionBody`'s labelled-section grammar carrying `Result` plus `Sent`, the prompt no card surfaced before. **The case this actually fixes:** `ResumeAgent` returns `success:false` for an unknown agent while the CALL succeeds (`ResumeAgentTool.tsx:123-129`), so the row status is `success` and the card read as a green `done` with the failure sealed in JSON. The peek tints from the ack's own `success`, never the row status; the header keeps saying `done` because that is true of the call. **Detection is shape-keyed, not name-keyed** (`toolAck.ts`, a pure `.ts` module so it is testable without a DOM against the SSR-only suite): boolean `success` + non-empty string `message`, fail-soft to null on every other shape. A correction worth recording — an earlier claim that Skill/Config/TaskUpdate share this shape was WRONG (they carry `success` but no `message` at all), so the peek reaches `ResumeAgent` and `SendMessage` only. `exact` splits the two renderings: the peek runs for any ack because it hides nothing, but the BODY is replaced only when `success`/`message` are the object's sole fields, so `SendMessage`'s optional `routing` (`SendMessageTool.ts:122-126`) can never vanish silently. **§0 flags:** ➕ the `'agent-control'` family and its hue are additions — the prototype's generic fallback is deliberately grey for a tool it does not recognise, but `ResumeAgent` is a KNOWN tool that merely lacked a case, and the hue is the prototype's own agent-family violet (`Messages.jsx` FE_FAMC `agent:'#a78bfa'`), left unused here because this app tokenised its agent family to `text-accent`; operator selected the mark/word/scope from three rendered options before any code was written. 🔁 the context figure stays inline prose: surfacing it as a stats line needs an additive `contextTokens`/`contextWindowTokens` pair on the tool's output schema, and regex-scraping it out of a sentence was refused. No protocol frame, preload channel, security boundary or locked decision touched; engine untouched. Evidence: `bun test app/renderer/src/TranscriptView.test.tsx app/renderer/src/toolAck.test.ts` **98 pass / 0 fail** · `bun test app/` **2273 pass / 0 fail** (168 files) · app tsc clean · sidecar wrapper green (5,559 upstream ignored) · hardening **19/19** · renderer build green · `#a78bfa` confirmed present in the built CSS so the Tailwind v4 dynamic-class trap is not in play · `git diff --check` clean · em-dash sweep leaves code comments only. **UNVERIFIED (operator GUI):** the card in a live transcript against a real resume. The suite is SSR-only, so a collapsed card was proven by asserting the ack text is present with `aria-expanded="false"`, which proves markup, not what the running app paints. |

Two fixes landed mid-sitting and are the highest-value outstanding checks, because both are
pure-interaction changes and the `app/` renderer suite is SSR-only — no headless test in this
repo can press a key, fire focus, or run an effect, so their whole interaction layer is unproven:

- **CC-13** Settings → Permissions (fix `59ca42e`) — open Settings → Permissions with **no session
  open**. The pane must render rules immediately instead of *"Waiting for the engine's permission
  context…"*, and the "Default mode" heading must show the saved `permissions.defaultMode`, not a
  session's live mode.
- **CC-16** composer during connect (fix `2f4278d`) — open a not-live session and **type
  immediately**: the first keystroke must land, with no `Focus to reconnect…` / `Connecting…` and
  no dropped characters. Press Enter *inside* the connect window: the turn must start once ready.
  Then mid-turn, the composer must still refuse input and the send arrow stay grey.
- **CC-18** workspace reorder (fix `78e920b`) — the drag gesture, real `localStorage`, and restart
  persistence are all unreachable from an SSR suite. With ≥3 workspace groups in the rail:
  (1) hover the rail open, press-and-hold a workspace HEADING (the label, not a session row) and
  drag it over another heading — a 2px accent rule must appear on the edge it will land on
  (below when dragging down, above when dragging up) and the dragged heading must dim; release and
  it must land exactly there, with its sessions intact. (2) Confirm nothing else moved: the other
  headings keep their relative order and no row inside any group changed position. (3) Type in the
  rail's search box until only two groups remain, reorder those two, clear the search — the
  filtered-out groups must reappear in their previous relative order, not reshuffled. (4) Click a
  session in the LAST group: its group must NOT jump to the top (CC-2 warp-free). (5) Quit and
  relaunch Cat Code — the custom order must be exactly as left. (6) Open the Sessions page — its
  workspace grouping must still be plain alphabetical, unaffected by the rail's order. (7) Keyboard
  path: Tab/click to focus a workspace heading and press **⌥↑ / ⌥↓** — it must move one slot per
  press and stop at the ends. (8) The **Unknown workspace** group (only present if some transcript
  has no recorded workspace) must stay pinned at the BOTTOM and refuse to drag. (9) Drag a TAB to a
  pane edge — the split drop must still work, unaffected by the new drag type.

## How to update this file (for the finishing session)
1. Flip the session's **Status** (⬜→🟡→✅) and add today's date + a one-line note.
2. If you just cleared a **phase gate**, say so in that phase's header.
3. If you generated the next phase's backlog, add its sessions as rows here **with 🧠 tags**.
